# Commands · D · part 2

1,460 entries, dead-code to define-governance. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dead-code**: Hostile hunt for code that is unreferenced or unreachable — with the deletion-safety discipline that no symbol is called dead until every reachability channel is ruled out. Assume the codebase hides both cruft that should be deleted and live code that only *looks* dead; prove which is which. Grep-shows-no-callers is a hypothesis, never a verdict. ([ivuorinen/skills](https://github.com/ivuorinen/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/nitpicker/commands/dead-code.md) · ★3)
- **dead-code**: Detects and safely removes unused exports, imports, files, and dependencies. Runs knip, verifies each finding, removes in small commits. ([NaviAndrei/AgentFlow-Studio](https://github.com/NaviAndrei/AgentFlow-Studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/dead-code.md) · ★1)
- **dead-code**: Find and remove dead code from the codebase. ([Sansar35/claude-agent](https://github.com/Sansar35/claude-agent/blob/HEAD/claude-config/commands/dead-code.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **dead-code**: Scan repo for unused files/functions and propose safe deletions with report and git patch ([Benny9193/devflow](https://github.com/Benny9193/devflow/blob/HEAD/commands/dead-code.md))
- **dead-code**: Read the root CLAUDE.md (auto-loaded) for architecture boundaries and module layout. Read each module's own CLAUDE.md before auditing that module. ([jobu711/options_arena](https://github.com/jobu711/options_arena/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/dead-code.md))
- **dead-code-apply**: Apply the dead code removal patch after review ([Benny9193/devflow](https://github.com/Benny9193/devflow/blob/HEAD/commands/dead-code-apply.md))

- **deadcode**: Find dead code with a configured Knip baseline, then delete it by category behind a ratchet ([kensaurus/cursor-kenji](https://github.com/kensaurus/cursor-kenji/blob/HEAD/commands/deadcode.md) · ★9)
- **deadcode**: Detect dead code (unused files, exports, types, and dependencies) in a JS/TS repository using Knip. Produces deadcode-report.html, deadcode-report.md, and deadcode-report.json locally with run history and delta tracking. Optional --fix opens a draft PR that safely removes the dead code via an isolated git worktree — your working tree is never touched. Usage: /deadcode [path] [--fix | --fix-dry-run] [options] ([xianix-team/plugins-official](https://github.com/xianix-team/plugins-official/blob/HEAD/plugins/deadcode-scanner/commands/deadcode.md) · ★1)
